# /learn: Extract Knowledge from Anthropic Skill Architecture Research Session

## Context

The previous session researched Anthropic's official 29-page "Complete Guide to Building Skills for Claude" and applied its recommendations to Cohezion's skill infrastructure. Two reusable patterns emerged that are worth capturing as project skills:

1. **PRIME skill migration workflow** — A repeatable, non-obvious workflow for upgrading Cohezion's 143 flat-file PRIME skills to Anthropic's spec (YAML frontmatter with the description formula). 10 skills were migrated; 133 remain.

2. **Skill trigger test suite pattern** — A YAML-based format for testing whether skills trigger at the right time, with should-trigger, should-not-trigger, and paraphrased variants.

No existing `.claude/skills/` entries cover either topic. Both patterns are verified (actually worked in session) and highly reusable.

## Skills to Create

### Skill 1: `prime-skill-migration`

**Location:** `.claude/skills/prime-skill-migration/SKILL.md`

**Purpose:** Document the repeatable workflow for adding Anthropic-spec YAML frontmatter to Cohezion's PRIME skills (133 remain).

**Key content:**

**YAML frontmatter template:**
```yaml
---
name: kebab-case-name          # required; no "claude"/"anthropic"; no XML brackets
description: |                 # required; max 1024 chars
  [What it does]. Use when [trigger conditions / user mentions X].
  Key capabilities: [list].
metadata:
  version: "1.0"
  legacy-name: ORIGINAL_PRIME_NAME
---
```

**Description formula** (Anthropic spec): `[What it does] + [When to use it] + [Key capabilities]`
- Include exact trigger phrases users would say (e.g., "compound engineering", "skill refinement")
- Name = kebab-case version of the PRIME skill concept (e.g., `COMPOUND_ENGINEERING_PRIME` → `compound-engineering`)

**Process:**
1. Read the PRIME skill file to understand its purpose
2. Draft `name` (kebab-case, no reserved words)
3. Draft `description` using the formula (<1024 chars)
4. Prepend frontmatter block before existing content
5. Verify with `grep "^name:" src/cohezion/skills/*.md | head -20`

**Remaining PRIME skills:** 133 files in `src/cohezion/skills/` without frontmatter (10 were migrated: compound-engineering, testing, security-guardrails, flume-methodology, token-efficiency, team-orchestration, reliability, self-healing, model-routing, semantic-caching).

---

### Skill 2: `skill-trigger-testing`

**Location:** `.claude/skills/skill-trigger-testing/SKILL.md`

**Purpose:** Document the YAML test suite pattern for validating skill trigger accuracy (should hit 90% accuracy per Anthropic spec).

**Test suite format** (file: `tests/skills/trigger_test_suites.yaml`):
```yaml
skills:
  - name: skill-name
    description: "Brief description"
    should_trigger:
      - "Direct phrase that should load this skill"
      - "Another triggering phrase"
    should_not_trigger:
      - "Unrelated phrase that must NOT load this skill"
    paraphrased:
      - "Reworded version of trigger that should still match"
```

**Validation:** `uv run python -c "import yaml; yaml.safe_load(open('tests/skills/trigger_test_suites.yaml'))" && echo "Valid YAML"`

**Target:** 5 trigger phrases, 5 non-trigger phrases, 3 paraphrased variants per skill.

---

## Verification

After creation:
```bash
# Confirm skills appear in Claude Code skill list
ls .claude/skills/
# Validate YAML frontmatter is parseable
uv run python -c "
import re, pathlib
for f in pathlib.Path('.claude/skills').rglob('SKILL.md'):
    text = f.read_text()
    match = re.match(r'^---\n(.*?)\n---', text, re.DOTALL)
    print(f.parent.name, '✓' if match else '✗ NO FRONTMATTER')
"
```

     1→# SKILL: BATCHING_PROTOCOL_PRIME
     2→
     3→## DOMAIN EXPERTISE
     4→Expert methodology for consolidating multiple independent, menial tasks into high-density prompts for local SLM execution, significantly reducing context load and token waste.
     5→
     6→## KEY TEXTS & CONCEPTS
     7→- **Task Consolidation**: The process of gathering multiple queries (e.g., "document A", "rephrase B") into a single prompt session.
     8→- **Density-Optimized Prompt**: A prompt structure that uses clear delimiters (`[TASK_ID: <id>]`) to help SLMs separate and process multiple instructions.
     9→- **Context Overhead**: The additional tokens consumed by repeating system prompts and truth anchors for multiple independent requests.
    10→- **Throughput vs. Latency**: Batching increases throughput (tasks per second) while slightly increasing latency per batch, but drastically reduces overall resource consumption.
    11→
    12→## INSTRUCTION
    13→1. **Identify Batch Candidates**: Target tasks with low complexity (menial) that don't require immediate real-time response.
    14→2. **Consolidate with Delimiters**:
    15→    - Use clear Task IDs.
```

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
The writing-skills guide emphasizes **CSO (Claude Search Optimization)**: descriptions should start with "Use when..." and describe *triggering conditions only*, never summarize the skill's workflow. This prevents Claude from taking a shortcut and following the description instead of reading the full skill body.

For these reference/technique skills, the key CSO factors are: concrete trigger phrases users would say, keyword coverage for discoverability, and accurate counts so future sessions don't work from stale data.
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

Now let me create both skill files. These are independent so I'll write them in parallel.

> TOOL

tool_use Write
id: toolu_01DE3mB3u2J2VzzKAU2QDiT1
```json
{
  "file_path": "/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/.claude/skills/prime-skill-migration/SKILL.md",
  "content": "---\nname: prime-skill-migration\ndescription: Use when migrating Cohezion PRIME skills to Anthropic spec, adding\n  YAML frontmatter to skill files, or when user mentions \"migrate skills\",\n  \"add frontmatter\", \"skill upgrade\", or \"PRIME migration\".\n---\n\n# PRIME Skill Migration\n\nRepeatable workflow for adding Anthropic-spec YAML frontmatter to Cohezion's\nflat-file PRIME skills in `src/cohezion/skills/`.\n\n## Status\n\n- **Migrated:** 18 skills (have `---` frontmatter block)\n- **Remaining:** ~72 skills (no frontmatter)\n- Check live: `grep -rL \"^---\" src/cohezion/skills/*PRIME*.md | wc -l`\n\n## YAML Frontmatter Template\n\nPrepend this block before the existing `# SKILL:` content:\n\n```yaml\n---\nname: kebab-case-name\ndescription: |\n  [What it does]. Use when [trigger conditions / user mentions X].\n  Key capabilities: [list].\nmetadata:\n  version: \"1.0\"\n  legacy-name: ORIGINAL_PRIME_NAME\n---\n```\n\n## Description Formula (Anthropic Spec)\n\n`[What it does] + [When to use it] + [Key capabilities]`\n\n- Max 1024 characters\n- Include exact trigger phrases users would say\n- No reserved words: avoid \"claude\", \"anthropic\", no XML brackets in name\n- Name = kebab-case of the PRIME concept (e.g., `BATCHING_PROTOCOL_PRIME` → `batching-protocol`)\n\n## Process\n\n1. **Read** the PRIME skill file — understand its purpose and domain\n2. **Draft name** — kebab-case, no reserved words, descriptive\n3. **Draft description** — use the formula, include trigger phrases, <1024 chars\n4. **Prepend** the frontmatter block before the existing `# SKILL:` heading\n5. **Verify** — `grep \"^name:\" src/cohezion/skills/*.md | head -20`\n\n## Common Mistakes\n\n| Mistake | Fix |\n|---------|-----|\n| Name uses underscores | Use hyphens: `batching-protocol` not `batching_protocol` |\n| Description too long | Cut to <1024 chars; focus on triggers, not full explanation |\n| Missing `legacy-name` | Always include original filename for traceability |\n| Frontmatter after content | Must be the very first thing in the file |\n\n## Quick Reference\n\nAlready migrated (don't re-do):\n`compound-engineering`, `testing`, `security-guardrails`, `flume-methodology`,\n`token-efficiency`, `team-orchestration`, `reliability`, `self-healing`,\n`model-routing`, `semantic-caching`, `capability-registry`,\n`computational-relativity`, `dissipative-structures`, `hiho-stability`,\n`holographic-flume`, `noether-conservation`, `physics-lineage`,\n`universe-simulation-persistence`\n"
}

```json
{
  "file_path": "/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/.claude/skills/skill-trigger-testing/SKILL.md",
  "content": "---\nname: skill-trigger-testing\ndescription: Use when creating trigger test suites for skills, validating skill\n  discovery accuracy, or when user mentions \"trigger tests\", \"skill testing\",\n  \"should trigger\", or \"trigger accuracy\".\n---\n\n# Skill Trigger Testing\n\nYAML-based test suite pattern for validating whether skills trigger at the\nright time. Target: 90% accuracy per Anthropic spec.\n\n## Test Suite Format\n\nFile location: `tests/skills/trigger_test_suites.yaml`\n\n```yaml\nskills:\n  - name: skill-name\n    description: \"Brief description of the skill\"\n    should_trigger:\n      - \"Direct phrase that should load this skill\"\n      - \"Another triggering phrase\"\n      - \"Third trigger variant\"\n      - \"Fourth trigger variant\"\n      - \"Fifth trigger variant\"\n    should_not_trigger:\n      - \"Unrelated phrase that must NOT load this skill\"\n      - \"Adjacent-domain phrase that should go elsewhere\"\n      - \"Ambiguous phrase that belongs to a different skill\"\n      - \"Generic request with no skill-specific signal\"\n      - \"Similar vocabulary but different intent\"\n    paraphrased:\n      - \"Reworded version of trigger that should still match\"\n      - \"Casual/informal way of asking for same thing\"\n      - \"Technical jargon variant of the trigger\"\n```\n\n## Coverage Targets\n\nPer skill:\n- **5** should-trigger phrases (exact user language)\n- **5** should-not-trigger phrases (adjacent but wrong)\n- **3** paraphrased variants (rewording of triggers)\n\n## Writing Good Test Cases\n\n### should_trigger\nUse exact phrases a user would type. Include:\n- Direct requests (\"migrate my PRIME skills\")\n- Symptom descriptions (\"skills don't have frontmatter\")\n- Domain keywords (\"add YAML metadata to skill files\")\n\n### should_not_trigger\nTarget adjacent domains that share vocabulary:\n- Same keywords, different intent\n- Related skills that could false-match\n- Generic requests without skill-specific signal\n\n### paraphrased\nRephrase triggers with different vocabulary:\n- Casual vs formal (\"fix skill headers\" vs \"add YAML frontmatter\")\n- Different verb choices (\"upgrade\" vs \"migrate\" vs \"convert\")\n- Abbreviated vs verbose\n\n## Validation\n\n```bash\n# Verify YAML is parseable\nuv run python -c \"import yaml; yaml.safe_load(open('tests/skills/trigger_test_suites.yaml'))\" && echo \"Valid YAML\"\n\n# Count coverage\nuv run python -c \"\nimport yaml\ndata = yaml.safe_load(open('tests/skills/trigger_test_suites.yaml'))\nfor s in data['skills']:\n    t = len(s.get('should_trigger', []))\n    n = len(s.get('should_not_trigger', []))\n    p = len(s.get('paraphrased', []))\n    status = 'OK' if t >= 5 and n >= 5 and p >= 3 else 'LOW'\n    print(f\\\"{s['name']}: trigger={t} not={n} para={p} [{status}]\\\")\n\"\n```\n\n## Common Mistakes\n\n| Mistake | Fix |\n|---------|-----|\n| Triggers too generic (\"help me\") | Use skill-specific vocabulary |\n| Not-triggers too obvious (\"make coffee\") | Target adjacent domains that share keywords |\n| Missing paraphrased variants | Add casual, formal, and jargon rewording |\n| YAML syntax errors | Always validate after editing |\n"
}
```

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
1. **CSO descriptions use "Use when..." pattern** — The Anthropic spec and the writing-skills guide both emphasize that skill descriptions should describe *triggering conditions* only. Including workflow summaries causes Claude to follow the description as a shortcut instead of reading the full skill body.

2. **Accurate counts matter** — The plan estimated 10 migrated / 133 remaining, but live data shows 18 migrated / 72 remaining. Skills that encode stale counts become misleading references. The `prime-skill-migration` skill includes a live-check command (`grep -rL "^---"`) so future sessions always get current numbers.

3. **Two skill types, two patterns** — `prime-skill-migration` is a *technique* skill (step-by-step repeatable process), while `skill-trigger-testing` is a *reference* skill (format specification + validation commands). The writing-skills guide distinguishes these because they need different testing approaches — technique skills test for correct application, reference skills test for correct retrieval.
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

> AGENT

Review posted successfully.

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
1. **PR scope mismatch is a common anti-pattern** — When a feature branch accumulates many commits, the PR description often only reflects the latest change. This makes reviews impossible because reviewers can't understand the full scope. The branch `feat/compound-elegant-simplification` has 20+ commits but the PR only describes the Serena removal.

2. **GitHub's diff API has a 300-file limit** — `gh pr diff` fails with HTTP 406 when a PR exceeds 300 files. You must use `gh api repos/.../pulls/.../files --paginate` to get the file list programmatically instead.

3. **File size limits catch architectural drift** — The 300-line soft limit and 500-line hard limit in CLAUDE.md aren't arbitrary. MCP server files growing to 2000+ lines indicates that handler logic, route definitions, and business logic are all mixed together. Each of those 6 files should be split into focused modules (routes, handlers, models, etc.).
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

2. **6 production files exceed the 500-line hard limit** — with `servers/bmad/server.py` at 2,005 lines being the worst offender, plus 20 more files over the 300-line soft limit

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
The pattern across all 6 files is similar: a single file containing models/classes + route handlers + server setup. The standard split is: **models** (dataclasses/Pydantic), **business logic** (engine classes), **routes** (HTTP handlers), and **server** (entrypoint/main). This is the same layering pattern used in most web frameworks.

For the BMAD server specifically (2005 lines), there are ~25 route handlers that follow a repetitive pattern — perfect candidates for grouping by domain (bmm, gds, cis, tea, bmb).
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

Let me read the key sections of the biggest file to plan the split.

     1→"""BMAD MCP Server - Core implementation.
     2→
     3→Port: 8361
     4→Provides: 108 BMAD commands, 28 agent prompts, workflow resources.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→import asyncio
    10→import logging
    11→import os
    12→import sys
    13→from pathlib import Path
    14→from typing import Any
    15→
    16→from aiohttp import web
    17→
    18→
    19→# Configure logging
    20→logging.basicConfig(
    21→    level=logging.INFO,
    22→    format="%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(name)s: %(message)s",
    23→    stream=sys.stderr,
    24→)
    25→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    26→
    27→# Configuration
    28→MCP_PORT = int(os.getenv("MCP_PORT", "8361"))
    29→REDIS_URL = os.getenv("REDIS_URL", "redis://localhost:6379")
    30→BMAD_DATA_PATH = Path(os.getenv("BMAD_DATA_PATH", "_bmad"))
    31→
    32→
    33→class BMADEngine:
    34→    """Simple BMAD engine for workflow and agent management."""
    35→
    36→    def __init__(self, data_path: Path):
    37→        self.data_path = data_path
    38→        self._modules: dict[str, Any] = {}
    39→        self._workflows: dict[str, Any] = {}
    40→        self._agents: dict[str, Any] = {}
    41→        self._load_index()
    42→
    43→    def _load_index(self) -> None:
    44→        """Build index of modules, workflows, and agents."""
    45→        if not self.data_path.exists():
    46→            logger.warning(f"BMAD data path not found: {self.data_path}")
    47→            return
    48→
    49→        for module_dir in self.data_path.iterdir():
    50→            if module_dir.is_dir() and not module_dir.name.startswith("_"):
    51→                module_name = module_dir.name
    52→                self._modules[module_name] = {"name": module_name}
    53→
    54→                # Index workflows
    55→                workflows_dir = module_dir / "workflows"
    56→                if workflows_dir.exists():
    57→                    for workflow_file in workflows_dir.rglob("*.md"):
    58→                        rel_path = workflow_file.relative_to(workflows_dir)
    59→                        workflow_id = f"{module_name}/{rel_path.with_suffix('')}"
    60→                        self._workflows[workflow_id] = {
    61→                            "id": workflow_id,
    62→                            "module": module_name,
    63→                            "path": str(workflow_file),
    64→                            "name": workflow_file.stem,
    65→                        }
    66→
    67→                # Index agents
    68→                agents_dir = module_dir / "agents"
    69→                if agents_dir.exists():
    70→                    for agent_file in agents_dir.glob("*.md"):
    71→                        agent_id = f"{module_name}-{agent_file.stem}"
    72→                        self._agents[agent_id] = {
    73→                            "id": agent_id,
    74→                            "module": module_name,
    75→                            "path": str(agent_file),
    76→                            "name": agent_file.stem,
    77→                        }
    78→
    79→        logger.info(
    80→            f"BMAD: {len(self._modules)} modules, {len(self._workflows)} workflows, {len(self._agents)} agents"
```

  1992→    await site.start()
  1993→
  1994→    logger.info(f"BMAD MCP Server running on http://localhost:{MCP_PORT}")
  1995→
  1996→    # Keep running
  1997→    while True:
  1998→        await asyncio.sleep(3600)
  1999→
  2000→
  2001→if __name__ == "__main__":
  2002→    try:
  2003→        asyncio.run(main())
  2004→    except KeyboardInterrupt:
  2005→        logger.info("BMAD MCP Server stopped")
  2006→
```

    80→            f"BMAD: {len(self._modules)} modules, {len(self._workflows)} workflows, {len(self._agents)} agents"
    81→        )
    82→
    83→    def list_modules(self) -> list[dict]:
    84→        """List all modules."""
    85→        return [{"name": name} for name in self._modules]
    86→
    87→    def list_workflows(self, module: str | None = None) -> list[dict]:
    88→        """List workflows."""
    89→        workflows = []
    90→        for wid, info in self._workflows.items():
    91→            if module and info["module"] != module:
    92→                continue
    93→            workflows.append({"id": wid, "module": info["module"], "name": info["name"]})
    94→        return workflows
    95→
    96→    def list_agents(self, module: str | None = None) -> list[dict]:
    97→        """List agents."""
    98→        agents = []
    99→        for aid, info in self._agents.items():
   100→            if module and info["module"] != module:
   101→                continue
   102→            agents.append({"id": aid, "module": info["module"], "name": info["name"]})
   103→        return agents
   104→
   105→    def get_workflow(self, workflow_id: str) -> dict:
   106→        """Get workflow content."""
   107→        if workflow_id not in self._workflows:
   108→            # Try to find by partial match
   109→            for wid, _ in self._workflows.items():
   110→                if workflow_id.lower() in wid.lower():
   111→                    workflow_id = wid
   112→                    break
   113→
   114→        if workflow_id in self._workflows:
   115→            info = self._workflows[workflow_id]
   116→            try:
   117→                content = Path(info["path"]).read_text()
   118→                return {"id": workflow_id, "content": content}
   119→            except Exception as e:
   120→                return {"error": f"Failed to read workflow: {e}"}
   121→
   122→        return {"error": f"Workflow not found: {workflow_id}"}
   123→
   124→    def get_agent(self, agent_id: str) -> dict:
   125→        """Get agent content."""
   126→        if agent_id not in self._agents:
   127→            # Try to find by partial match
   128→            for aid, _ in self._agents.items():
   129→                if agent_id.lower() in aid.lower():
   130→                    agent_id = aid
   131→                    break
   132→
   133→        if agent_id in self._agents:
   134→            info = self._agents[agent_id]
   135→            try:
   136→                content = Path(info["path"]).read_text()
   137→                return {"id": agent_id, "content": content}
   138→            except Exception as e:
   139→                return {"error": f"Failed to read agent: {e}"}
   140→
   141→        return {"error": f"Agent not found: {agent_id}"}
   142→
   143→    def get_next_steps(self, query: str) -> list[dict]:
   144→        """Get recommended next steps."""
   145→        query_lower = query.lower()
   146→        suggestions = []
   147→
   148→        # Pattern matching for suggestions
   149→        if any(kw in query_lower for kw in ["prd", "product"]):
   150→            suggestions.append(
   151→                {
   152→                    "command": "bmad_bmm_create_prd",
   153→                    "description": "Create a Product Requirements Document",
   154→                }
   155→            )
   156→
   157→        if any(kw in query_lower for kw in ["story", "user story"]):
   158→            suggestions.append(
   159→                {
   160→                    "command": "bmad_bmm_create_story",
   161→                    "description": "Create a user story",
   162→                }
   163→            )
   164→
   165→        if any(kw in query_lower for kw in ["sprint", "planning"]):
   166→            suggestions.append(
   167→                {
   168→                    "command": "bmad_bmm_sprint_planning",
   169→                    "description": "Plan a sprint",
   170→                }
   171→            )
   172→
   173→        if any(kw in query_lower for kw in ["game", "gdd"]):
   174→            suggestions.append(
   175→                {
   176→                    "command": "bmad_gds_create_game_brief",
   177→                    "description": "Create a game design brief",
   178→                }
   179→            )
   180→
   181→        if any(kw in query_lower for kw in ["brainstorm", "creative"]):
   182→            suggestions.append(
   183→                {
   184→                    "command": "bmad_cis_brainstorming",
   185→                    "description": "Facilitate brainstorming session",
   186→                }
   187→            )
   188→
   189→        if any(kw in query_lower for kw in ["test", "testing"]):
   190→            suggestions.append(
   191→                {
   192→                    "command": "bmad_tea_test_design",
   193→                    "description": "Design tests",
   194→                }
   195→            )
   196→
   197→        if any(kw in query_lower for kw in ["agent", "create agent"]):
   198→            suggestions.append(
   199→                {
   200→                    "command": "bmad_bmb_create_agent",
   201→                    "description": "Create a custom BMAD agent",
   202→                }
   203→            )
   204→
   205→        # Default suggestions
   206→        if not suggestions:
   207→            suggestions = [
   208→                {"command": "bmad_help", "description": "Get help with BMAD"},
   209→                {"command": "bmad_list_workflows", "description": "List available workflows"},
   210→                {"command": "bmad_list_agents", "description": "List available agents"},
   211→            ]
   212→
   213→        return suggestions
   214→
   215→
   216→# Global engine instance
   217→_engine: BMADEngine | None = None
   218→
   219→
   220→def get_engine() -> BMADEngine:
   221→    """Get or create BMAD engine."""
   222→    global _engine
   223→    if _engine is None:
   224→        _engine = BMADEngine(BMAD_DATA_PATH)
   225→    return _engine
   226→
   227→
   228→# HTTP API routes
   229→routes = web.RouteTableDef()

Good — I now have the full picture. Let me create the split files. The approach:

1. **`_shared.py`** — `routes`, `get_engine`, `BMADEngine`, config, logger
2. **`routes_bmm.py`** — BMM tool routes (core: create_prd through retrospective)
3. **`routes_bmm_ops.py`** — BMM ops routes (release_planning through performance_optimization)
4. **`routes_gds.py`** — GDS tool routes
5. **`routes_cis.py`** — CIS tool routes
6. **`routes_tea.py`** — TEA tool routes
7. **`routes_bmb.py`** — BMB tool routes
8. **`routes_general.py`** — General/utility routes (search, recommend, analyze, etc.)
9. **`server.py`** — Slim entrypoint (health, index, resource routes, create_app, main)

  1233→# =============================================================================
  1234→# RESOURCES API
  1235→# =============================================================================
  1236→# RESOURCES API
  1237→# =============================================================================
  1238→
  1239→
  1240→@routes.get("/resources/workflows/{module}/{path:.*}")
  1241→async def get_workflow_resource(request: web.Request) -> web.Response:
  1242→    """Get workflow content."""
  1243→    module = request.match_info["module"]
  1244→    path = request.match_info["path"]
  1245→
  1246→    engine = get_engine()
  1247→    workflow = engine.get_workflow(f"{module}/{path}")
  1248→
  1249→    if "error" in workflow:
  1250→        return web.json_response(workflow, status=404)
  1251→
  1252→    return web.json_response(workflow)
  1253→
  1254→
  1255→@routes.get("/resources/agents/{agent_id}")
  1256→async def get_agent_resource(request: web.Request) -> web.Response:
  1257→    """Get agent content."""
  1258→    agent_id = request.match_info["agent_id"]
  1259→
  1260→    engine = get_engine()
  1261→    agent = engine.get_agent(agent_id)
  1262→
  1263→    if "error" in agent:
  1264→        return web.json_response(agent, status=404)
  1265→
  1266→    return web.json_response(agent)
  1267→
  1268→
  1269→@routes.get("/resources/modules")
  1270→async def list_modules_resource(request: web.Request) -> web.Response:
  1271→    """List all modules."""
  1272→    engine = get_engine()
  1273→    return web.json_response({"modules": engine.list_modules()})
  1274→

  "content": "\"\"\"BMAD MCP Server - Shared config, engine, and route definitions.\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport logging\nimport os\nimport sys\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Any\n\nfrom aiohttp import web\n\n\nlogging.basicConfig(\n    level=logging.INFO,\n    format=\"%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(name)s: %(message)s\",\n    stream=sys.stderr,\n)\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\nMCP_PORT = int(os.getenv(\"MCP_PORT\", \"8361\"))\nREDIS_URL = os.getenv(\"REDIS_URL\", \"redis://localhost:6379\")\nBMAD_DATA_PATH = Path(os.getenv(\"BMAD_DATA_PATH\", \"_bmad\"))\n\nroutes = web.RouteTableDef()\n\n\nclass BMADEngine:\n    \"\"\"Simple BMAD engine for workflow and agent management.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, data_path: Path):\n        self.data_path = data_path\n        self._modules: dict[str, Any] = {}\n        self._workflows: dict[str, Any] = {}\n        self._agents: dict[str, Any] = {}\n        self._load_index()\n\n    def _load_index(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Build index of modules, workflows, and agents.\"\"\"\n        if not self.data_path.exists():\n            logger.warning(f\"BMAD data path not found: {self.data_path}\")\n            return\n\n        for module_dir in self.data_path.iterdir():\n            if module_dir.is_dir() and not module_dir.name.startswith(\"_\"):\n                module_name = module_dir.name\n                self._modules[module_name] = {\"name\": module_name}\n\n                workflows_dir = module_dir / \"workflows\"\n                if workflows_dir.exists():\n                    for workflow_file in workflows_dir.rglob(\"*.md\"):\n                        rel_path = workflow_file.relative_to(workflows_dir)\n                        workflow_id = f\"{module_name}/{rel_path.with_suffix('')}\"\n                        self._workflows[workflow_id] = {\n                            \"id\": workflow_id,\n                            \"module\": module_name,\n                            \"path\": str(workflow_file),\n                            \"name\": workflow_file.stem,\n                        }\n\n                agents_dir = module_dir / \"agents\"\n                if agents_dir.exists():\n                    for agent_file in agents_dir.glob(\"*.md\"):\n                        agent_id = f\"{module_name}-{agent_file.stem}\"\n                        self._agents[agent_id] = {\n                            \"id\": agent_id,\n                            \"module\": module_name,\n                            \"path\": str(agent_file),\n                            \"name\": agent_file.stem,\n                        }\n\n        logger.info(\n            f\"BMAD: {len(self._modules)} modules, {len(self._workflows)} workflows, {len(self._agents)} agents\"\n        )\n\n    def list_modules(self) -> list[dict]:\n        \"\"\"List all modules.\"\"\"\n        return [{\"name\": name} for name in self._modules]\n\n    def list_workflows(self, module: str | None = None) -> list[dict]:\n        \"\"\"List workflows.\"\"\"\n        workflows = []\n        for wid, info in self._workflows.items():\n            if module and info[\"module\"] != module:\n                continue\n            workflows.append({\"id\": wid, \"module\": info[\"module\"], \"name\": info[\"name\"]})\n        return workflows\n\n    def list_agents(self, module: str | None = None) -> list[dict]:\n        \"\"\"List agents.\"\"\"\n        agents = []\n        for aid, info in self._agents.items():\n            if module and info[\"module\"] != module:\n                continue\n            agents.append({\"id\": aid, \"module\": info[\"module\"], \"name\": info[\"name\"]})\n        return agents\n\n    def get_workflow(self, workflow_id: str) -> dict:\n        \"\"\"Get workflow content.\"\"\"\n        if workflow_id not in self._workflows:\n            for wid, _ in self._workflows.items():\n                if workflow_id.lower() in wid.lower():\n                    workflow_id = wid\n                    break\n\n        if workflow_id in self._workflows:\n            info = self._workflows[workflow_id]\n            try:\n                content = Path(info[\"path\"]).read_text()\n                return {\"id\": workflow_id, \"content\": content}\n            except Exception as e:\n                return {\"error\": f\"Failed to read workflow: {e}\"}\n\n        return {\"error\": f\"Workflow not found: {workflow_id}\"}\n\n    def get_agent(self, agent_id: str) -> dict:\n        \"\"\"Get agent content.\"\"\"\n        if agent_id not in self._agents:\n            for aid, _ in self._agents.items():\n                if agent_id.lower() in aid.lower():\n                    agent_id = aid\n                    break\n\n        if agent_id in self._agents:\n            info = self._agents[agent_id]\n            try:\n                content = Path(info[\"path\"]).read_text()\n                return {\"id\": agent_id, \"content\": content}\n            except Exception as e:\n                return {\"error\": f\"Failed to read agent: {e}\"}\n\n        return {\"error\": f\"Agent not found: {agent_id}\"}\n\n    def get_next_steps(self, query: str) -> list[dict]:\n        \"\"\"Get recommended next steps.\"\"\"\n        query_lower = query.lower()\n        suggestions = []\n\n        keyword_map = {\n            (\"prd\", \"product\"): (\"bmad_bmm_create_prd\", \"Create a Product Requirements Document\"),\n            (\"story\", \"user story\"): (\"bmad_bmm_create_story\", \"Create a user story\"),\n            (\"sprint\", \"planning\"): (\"bmad_bmm_sprint_planning\", \"Plan a sprint\"),\n            (\"game\", \"gdd\"): (\"bmad_gds_create_game_brief\", \"Create a game design brief\"),\n            (\"brainstorm\", \"creative\"): (\"bmad_cis_brainstorming\", \"Facilitate brainstorming session\"),\n            (\"test\", \"testing\"): (\"bmad_tea_test_design\", \"Design tests\"),\n            (\"agent\", \"create agent\"): (\"bmad_bmb_create_agent\", \"Create a custom BMAD agent\"),\n        }\n\n        for keywords, (command, description) in keyword_map.items():\n            if any(kw in query_lower for kw in keywords):\n                suggestions.append({\"command\": command, \"description\": description})\n\n        if not suggestions:\n            suggestions = [\n                {\"command\": \"bmad_help\", \"description\": \"Get help with BMAD\"},\n                {\"command\": \"bmad_list_workflows\", \"description\": \"List available workflows\"},\n                {\"command\": \"bmad_list_agents\", \"description\": \"List available agents\"},\n            ]\n\n        return suggestions\n\n\n_engine: BMADEngine | None = None\n\n\ndef get_engine() -> BMADEngine:\n    \"\"\"Get or create BMAD engine.\"\"\"\n    global _engine\n    if _engine is None:\n        _engine = BMADEngine(BMAD_DATA_PATH)\n    return _engine\n"
}

def tool_bmad_status(request: web.Request) -> web.Response:\n    \"\"\"Status tool.\"\"\"\n    try:\n        data = await request.json()\n        engine = get_engine()\n        return web.json_response(\n            {\n                \"tool\": \"bmad_status\",\n                \"version\": \"6.0.4\",\n                \"modules\": [m[\"name\"] for m in engine.list_modules()],\n  except Exception as e:\n        return web.json_response({\"error\": str(e)}, status=500)\n\n\n@routes.post(\"/tools/bmad_doc_retrieve\")\nasync def tool_bmad_doc_retrieve(request: web.Request) -> web.Response:\n    \"\"\"Retrieve documentation with token-efficient chunks.\"\"\"\n    try:\n        data = await request.json()\n        library = data.get(\"library\", \"\")\n        query = data.get(\"query\", \"\")\n\n        if not query:\n            return web.json_response({\"error\": \"Query is required\"}, status=400)\n\n        chunks = [\n            {\n                \"content\": f\"Relevant documentation for '{query}' from {library}...\",\n                \"source\": f\"{library}/workflows/relevant.md\",\n                \"token_count\": 150,\n                \"relevance_score\": 0.94,\n            }\n        ]\n        total_tokens = sum(c[\"token_count\"] for c in chunks)\n\n        return web.json_response(\n            {\n                \"tool\": \"bmad_doc_retrieve\",\n                \"library\": library,\n                \"query\": query,\n                \"chunks\": chunks,\n                \"chunk_count\": len(chunks),\n                \"total_tokens\": total_tokens,\n                \"source\": \"local\",\n                \"message\": f\"Retrieved {len(chunks)} chunks ({total_tokens} tokens)\",\n            }\n        )\n    except Exception as e:\n        return web.json_response({\"error\": str(e)}, status=500)\n"
}

routes\n\n\n@routes.post(\"/tools/bmad_search\")\nasync def tool_bmad_search(request: web.Request) -> web.Response:\n    \"\"\"Search across BMAD content.\"\"\"\n    try:\n        data = await request.json()\n        query = data.get(\"query\", \"\")\n        content_type = data.get(\"type\", \"all\")\n\n        engine = get_engine()\n        results = []\n\n        if content_type in [\"all\", \"workflows\"]:\n            for wf in engine.list_workflows():\n                if query.lower() in wf[\"id\"].lower() or query.lower() in wf[\"name\"].lower():\n                    results.append({\"type\": \"workflow\", **wf})\n\n        if content_type in [\"all\", \"agents\"]:\n            for agent in engine.list_agents():\n                if query.lower() in agent[\"id\"].lower() or query.lower() in agent[\"name\"].lower():\n                    results.append({\"type\": \"agent\", **agent})\n\n        return web.json_response(\n            {\n                \"tool\": \"bmad_search\",\n                \"query\": query,\n                \"type\": content_type,\n                \"count\": len(results),\n                \"results\": results[:20],\n            }\n        )\n    except Exception as e:\n        return web.json_response({\"error\": str(e)}, status=500)\n\n\n@routes.post(\"/tools/bmad_recommend\")\nasync def tool_bmad_recommend(request: web.Request) -> web.Response:\n    \"\"\"Get BMAD recommendations based on context.\"\"\"\n    try:\n        data = await request.json()\n        context = data.get(\"context\", \"\")\n        context_lower = context.lower()\n        recommendations = []\n\n        keyword_map = {\n            (\"product\", \"prd\", \"requirement\"): (\"bmad_bmm_create_prd\", \"Creating product requirements\"),\n            (\"test\", \"testing\", \"qa\"): (\"bmad_tea_test_design\", \"Testing context detected\"),\n            (\"game\", \"design\", \"player\"): (\"bmad_gds_create_game_brief\", \"Game development context\"),\n            (\"brainstorm\", \"ideate\", \"creative\"): (\"bmad_cis_brainstorming\", \"Creative ideation needed\"),\n        }\n\n        for keywords, (tool, reason) in keyword_map.items():\n            if any(word in context_lower for word in keywords):\n                recommendations.append({\"tool\": tool, \"reason\": \"context\": context[:100],\n                \"recommendations\": recommendations,\n                \"count\": len(recommendations),\n            }\n        )\n    except Exception as e:\n \"bmad_quick_start\",\n                \"goal\": goal,\n                \"path\": paths.get(goal, paths[\"new_project\"]),\n                \"estimated_time\": \"30 minutes\",\n            }\n web.Request) -> web.Response:\n    \"\"\"Export session data.\"\"\"\n    try:\n        data = await request.json()\n        session_id = data.get(\"session_id\", \"\")\n        return web.json_response(\n            {\n                \"tool\": \"bmad_export_session\",\n                \"session_id\": session_id,\n                \"format\": data.get(\"format\", \"json\"),\n here\",\n                },\n            }\n        )\n    except Exception as e:\n        return web.json_response({\"error\": str(e)}, status=500)\n\n\n@routes.post(\"/tools/bmad_import_session\")\nasync def tool_bmad_import_session(request: web.Request) -> web.Response:\n    \"\"\"Import session data.\"\"\"\n    try:\n        data = await request.json()\n        import_data = data.get(\"data\", {})\n        return web.json_response(\n            {\n                \"tool\": \"bmad_import_session\",\n                \"imported\": True,\n                \"session_id\": import_data.get(\"session_id\", \"new-session\"),\n                \"message\": \"Session imported successfully\",\n            }\n        )\n    except Exception as e:\n        return web.json_response({\"error\": str(e)}, status=500)\n"
}

  "content": "\"\"\"BMAD MCP Server - Entrypoint.\n\nPort: 8361\nProvides: 90 BMAD tools, agent prompts, workflow resources.\nRoutes are split by domain into routes_*.py modules.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport asyncio\n\nfrom aiohttp import web\n\nfrom ._shared import MCP_PORT, get_engine, logger, routes\n\n# Import route modules to register their @routes decorators\nfrom . import routes_bmb  # noqa: F401\nfrom . import routes_bmm  # noqa: F401\nfrom . import routes_bmm_ops  # noqa: F401\nfrom . import routes_cis  # noqa: F401\nfrom . import routes_gds  # noqa: F401\nfrom . import routes_general  # noqa: F401\nfrom . import routes_tea  # noqa: F401\n\n\n@routes.get(\"/health\")\nasync def health(request: web.Request) -> web.Response:\n    \"\"\"Health check endpoint.\"\"\"\n    return web.json_response({\"status\": \"healthy\", \"server\": \"bmad\", \"port\": MCP_PORT})\n\n\n@routes.get(\"/\")\nasync def index(request: web.Request) -> web.Response:\n    \"\"\"Server info endpoint.\"\"\"\n    engine = get_engine()\n    return web.json_response(\n        {\n            \"server\": \"BMAD MCP Server\",\n            \"version\": \"6.0.4\",\n            \"port\": MCP_PORT,\n            \"modules\": [m[\"name\"] for m in engine.list_modules()],\n            \"workflows_count\": len(engine._workflows),\n            \"agents_count\": len(engine._agents),\n            \"tools_count\": 90,\n        }\n    )\n\n\n@routes.get(\"/resources/workflows/{module}/{path:.*}\")\nasync def get_workflow_resource(request: web.Request) -> web.Response:\n    \"\"\"Get workflow content.\"\"\"\n    module = request.match_info[\"module\"]\n    path = request.match_info[\"path\"]\n    engine = get_engine()\n    workflow = engine.get_workflow(f\"{module}/{path}\")\n    if \"error\" in workflow:\n        return web.json_response(workflow, status=404)\n    return web.json_response(workflow)\n\n\n@routes.get(\"/resources/agents/{agent_id}\")\nasync def get_agent_resource(request: web.Request) -> web.Response:\n    \"\"\"Get agent content.\"\"\"\n    agent_id = request.match_info[\"agent_id\"]\n    engine = get_engine()\n    agent = engine.get_agent(agent_id)\n    if \"error\" in agent:\n        return web.json_response(agent, status=404)\n    return web.json_response(agent)\n\n\n@routes.get(\"/resources/modules\")\nasync def list_modules_resource(request: web.Request) -> web.Response:\n    \"\"\"List all modules.\"\"\"\n    engine = get_engine()\n    return web.json_response({\"modules\": engine.list_modules()})\n\n\ndef create_app() -> web.Application:\n    \"\"\"Create the web application.\"\"\"\n    from cohezion.mcp.shared.auth import api_key_middleware\n\n    app = web.Application(middlewares=[api_key_middleware])\n    app.add_routes(routes)\n    return app\n\n\napp = create_app()\n\n\nasync def main():\n    \"\"\"Run the BMAD MCP Server.\"\"\"\n    get_engine()\n    logger.info(f\"Starting BMAD MCP Server on port {MCP_PORT}\")\n    runner = web.AppRunner(app)\n    await runner.setup()\n    site = web.TCPSite(runner, \"0.0.0.0\", MCP_PORT)\n    await site.start()\n    logger.info(f\"BMAD MCP Server running on http://localhost:{MCP_PORT}\")\n    while True:\n        await asyncio.sleep(3600)\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    try:\n        asyncio.run(main())\n    except KeyboardInterrupt:\n        logger.info(\"BMAD MCP Server stopped\")\n"
}

> AGENT

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
The bmad/server.py split demonstrates the **route module pattern** for aiohttp: a `_shared.py` module defines the `RouteTableDef()` and shared engine, then each domain module imports and decorates with `@routes.post(...)`. The key trick is that `server.py` (the entrypoint) must import all route modules so their decorators execute and register on the shared `routes` object. The `# noqa: F401` comments tell ruff these "unused" imports are intentional side-effect imports.
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

Now let me tackle the remaining 5 files. Let me read them in parallel and do the splits.

   522→@app.tool()
   523→async def bmad_party_mode(
   524→    objective: str,
   525→    agents: list[str],
   526→    duration_minutes: int = 30,
   527→    session_id: str = "",
   528→) -> dict[str, Any]:
   529→    """Multi-agent collaboration mode. Use for complex tasks requiring multiple perspectives.
   530→
   531→    Args:
   532→        objective: What the group should accomplish
   533→        agents: List of agent names to include
   534→        duration_minutes: Time limit for session
   535→        session_id: Optional session ID
   536→
   537→    Returns:
   538→        Party mode session setup and instructions
   539→    """
   540→    engine = get_engine()
   541→
   542→    # Load all requested agents
   543→    agent_personas = []
   544→    for agent_name in agents:
   545→        persona = engine.load_agent(agent_name)
   546→        if persona:
   547→            agent_personas.append(persona)
   548→
   549→    return {
   550→        "party_session": {
   551→            "objective": objective,
   552→            "agents": agent_personas,
   553→            "duration": duration_minutes,
   554→            "facilitator_notes": f"Guide {len(agents)} agents to collaborate on: {objective}",
   555→        },
   556→        "workflow": "core/workflows/party-mode.md",
   557→        "agent_count": len(agent_personas),
   558→        "session_id": session_id or engine.generate_session_id(),
   559→    }

   639→async def bmad_status(

   641→) -> dict[str, Any]:

   644→    Args:

   657→            "history": session.get("history", []) if session else [],
   658→        }
   659→    else:
   660→        # Return general status
   661→        engine = get_engine()
   662→        return {
   663→            "modules_loaded": engine.list_modules(),
   664→            "agents_available": len(engine.list_agents()),
   665→            "workflows_available": len(engine.list_workflows()),
   666→            "redis_connected": session_mgr.is_connected(),

  "content": "\"\"\"BMAD MCP Server - FastMCP app, config, and engine accessors.\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport logging\nimport os\nimport sys\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom fastmcp import FastMCP\n\nfrom cohezion.mcp.servers.bmad.engine import BMADEngine\nfrom cohezion.mcp.shared.session import SessionManager\n\n\nlogging.basicConfig(\n    level=logging.INFO,\n    format=\"%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(name)s: %(message)s\",\n    stream=sys.stderr,\n)\nlogger = logging.getLogger(\"bmad-mcp\")\n\napp = FastMCP(\"bmad-method\")\n\nBMAD_DATA_PATH = Path(os.getenv(\"BMAD_DATA_PATH\", \"_bmad\"))\nREDIS_URL = os.getenv(\"REDIS_URL\", \"redis://localhost:6379\")\n\n_engine: BMADEngine | None = None\n_session_manager: SessionManager | None = None\n\n\ndef get_engine() -> BMADEngine:\n    \"\"\"Get or create BMAD engine.\"\"\"\n    global _engine\n    if _engine is None:\n        _engine = BMADEngine(BMAD_DATA_PATH)\n        logger.info(f\"BMAD engine initialized with data from {BMAD_DATA_PATH}\")\n    return _engine\n\n\ndef get_session_manager() -> SessionManager:\n    \"\"\"Get or create session manager.\"\"\"\n    global _session_manager\n    if _session_manager is None:\n        _session_manager = SessionManager(REDIS_URL)\n        logger.info(f\"Session manager initialized with Redis at {REDIS_URL}\")\n    return _session_manager\n"
}

  "content": "\"\"\"BMAD MCP tools - core BMAD commands via FastMCP.\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom typing import Any\n\nfrom cohezion.mcp.bmad_app import app, get_engine, get_session_manager\n\n\n@app.tool()\nasync def bmad_help(\n    query: str = \"\",\n    context: str = \"\",\n    session_id: str = \"\",\n) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Interactive BMAD help system. Use when user asks 'what should I do next'.\n\n    Args:\n        query: User's question or current situation\n        context: Current project context/state\n        session_id: Optional session ID for continuity\n    \"\"\"\n    engine = get_engine()\n    session = await get_session_manager().get_session(session_id) if session_id else None\n    _help_workflow = engine.load_workflow(\"core\", \"tasks/help\")\n    analysis = engine.analyze_context(context, session)\n    recommendations = engine.get_next_steps(query, analysis, session)\n    return {\n        \"help_response\": recommendations,\n        \"workflow_loaded\": \"core/tasks/help.md\",\n        \"analysis\": analysis,\n        \"available_modules\": engine.list_modules(),\n        \"suggested_commands\": recommendations.get(\"suggested_commands\", []),\n    }\n\n\n@app.tool()\nasync def bmad_bmm_create_prd(\n    product_idea: str,\n    target_users: str,\n    key_features: list[str],\n    session_id: str = \"\",\n    save_to_vault: bool = True,\n) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Create a Product Requirements Document (PRD). Use when starting a new product.\n\n    Args:\n        product_idea: Description of the product or feature\n        target_users: Who will use this product\n        key_features: List of key features/capabilities\n        session_id: Optional session ID\n        save_to_vault: Whether to save the PRD to vault\n    \"\"\"\n    engine = get_engine()\n    workflow = engine.load_workflow(\"bmm\", \"2-plan-workflows/create-prd/workflow-create-prd\")\n    prd_content = await engine.execute_workflow(\n        workflow,\n        {\"product_idea\": product_idea, \"target_users\": target_users, \"key_features\": key_features},\n        session_id,\n    )\n    if session_id:\n        await get_session_manager().update_session(\n            session_id, {\"last_action\": \"create_prd\", \"prd_created\": True}\n        )\n    return {\n        \"prd_content\": prd_content,\n        \"workflow\": \"bmm/2-plan-workflows/create-prd/workflow-create-prd.md\",\n        \"next_steps\": [\n            \"Review and refine the PRD\",\n            \"Use bmad_bmm_validate_prd to validate\",\n            \"Use bmad_bmm_create_architecture to design architecture\",\n        ],\n        \"session_id\": session_id,\n    }\n\n\n@app.tool()\nasync def bmad_bmm_create_story(\n    story_title: str,\n    acceptance_criteria: list[str],\n    priority: str = \"medium\",\n    points: int = 3,\n    session_id: str = \"\",\n) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Create a user story. Use when breaking down features into implementable stories.\n\n    Args:\n        story_title: Clear, actionable story title\n        acceptance_criteria: List of criteria for story completion\n        priority: Story priority (critical/high/medium/low)\n        points: Story points estimate\n        session_id: Optional session ID\n    \"\"\"\n    engine = get_engine()\n    workflow = engine.load_workflow(\"bmm\", \"2-plan-workflows/create-story\")\n    story = await engine.execute_workflow(\n        workflow,\n        {\"title\": story_title, \"acceptance_criteria\": acceptance_criteria,\n         \"priority\": priority, \"points\": points},\n        session_id,\n    )\n    return {\n        \"story\": story,\n        \"workflow\": \"bmm/2-plan-workflows/create-story.md\",\n        \"next_steps\": [\"Add to sprint\", \"Assign to developer\", \"Break down into tasks\"],\n    }\n\n\n@app.tool()\nasync def bmad_bmm_sprint_planning(\n    stories: list[dict],\n    sprint_goal: str,\n    capacity: int,\n    session_id: str = \"\",\n) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Plan a sprint. Use when starting a new iteration.\n\n    Args:\n        stories: List of stories to consider\n        sprint_goal: Clear sprint objective\n        capacity: Team capacity in story points\n        session_id: Optional session ID\n    \"\"\"\n    engine = get_engine()\n    workflow = engine.load_workflow(\"bmm\", \"2-plan-workflows/sprint-planning\")\n    plan = await engine.execute_workflow(\n        workflow,\n        {\"stories\": stories, \"sprint_goal\": sprint_goal, \"capacity\": capacity},\n        session_id,\n    )\n    return {\n        \"sprint_plan\": plan,\n        \"workflow\": \"bmm/2-plan-workflows/sprint-planning.md\",\n        \"capacity_utilization\": plan.get(\"total_points\", 0) / capacity if capacity > 0 else 0,\n    }\n\n\n@app.tool()\nasync def bmad_bmm_dev_story(\n    story_id: str,\n    tech_stack: str,\n    existing_code: str = \"\",\n    session_id: str = \"\",\n) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Develop a user story. Use when implementing a story.\n\n    Args:\n        story_id: Story identifier\n        tech_stack: Technology stack being used\n        existing_code: Reference to existing codebase\n        session_id: Optional session ID\n    \"\"\"\n    engine = get_engine()\n    workflow = engine.load_workflow(\"bmm\", \"3-solutioning/dev-story\")\n    result = await engine.execute_workflow(\n        workflow,\n        {\"story_id\": story_id, \"tech_stack\": tech_stack, \"existing_code\": existing_code},\n        session_id,\n    )\n    return {\n        \"implementation_plan\": result,\n        \"workflow\": \"bmm/3-solutioning/dev-story.md\",\n        \"suggested_files\": result.get(\"files_to_create\", []),\n    }\n\n\n@app.tool()\nasync def bmad_bmm_code_review(\n    code_changes: str,\n    review_type: str = \"general\",\n    focus_areas: list[str] | None = None,\n    session_id: str = \"\",\n) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Review code changes. Use for quality assurance.\n\n    Args:\n        code_changes: Code to review (diff or full files)\n        review_type: Type of review (general/security/performance/refactor)\n        focus_areas: Specific areas to focus on\n        session_id: Optional session ID\n    \"\"\"\n    engine = get_engine()\n    workflow = engine.load_workflow(\"bmm\", \"4-implementation/code-review\")\n    review = await engine.execute_workflow(\n        workflow,\n        {\"code_changes\": code_changes, \"review_type\": review_type,\n         \"focus_areas\": focus_areas or []},\n        session_id,\n    )\n    return {\n        \"review_feedback\": review,\n        \"workflow\": \"bmm/4-implementation/code-review.md\",\n        \"issues_found\": len(review.get(\"issues\", [])),\n        \"suggestions_count\": len(review.get(\"suggestions\", [])),\n    }\n\n\n@app.tool()\nasync def bmad_gds_create_game_brief(\n    game_concept: str,\n    target_platform: str,\n    genre: str,\n    session_id: str = \"\",\n) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Create a game design brief. Use when starting game development.\n\n    Args:\n        game_concept: Core game concept and vision\n        target_platform: Platform (PC/console/mobile/VR)\n        genre: Game genre\n        session_id: Optional session ID\n    \"\"\"\n    engine = get_engine()\n    workflow = engine.load_workflow(\"gds\", \"workflows/create-game-brief\")\n    brief = await engine.execute_workflow(\n        workflow,\n        {\"game_concept\": game_concept, \"target_platform\": target_platform, \"genre\": genre},\n        session_id,\n    )\n    return {\n        \"game_brief\": brief,\n        \"workflow\": \"gds/workflows/create-game-brief.md\",\n        \"next_steps\": [\"Create GDD\", \"Design game architecture\", \"Prototype core loop\"],\n    }\n\n\n@app.tool()\nasync def bmad_gds_game_architecture(\n    game_brief_id: str,\n    engine_choice: str = \"\",\n    multiplayer: bool = False,\n    session_id: str = \"\",\n) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Design game architecture. Use after creating game brief.\n\n    Args:\n        game_brief_id: Reference to game brief\n        engine_choice: Game engine (Unity/Unreal/Godot/custom)\n        multiplayer: Whether game has multiplayer\n        session_id: Optional session ID\n    \"\"\"\n    engine = get_engine()\n    workflow = engine.load_workflow(\"gds\", \"workflows/game-architecture\")\n    arch = await engine.execute_workflow(\n        workflow,\n        {\"game_brief_id\": game_brief_id, \"engine_choice\": engine_choice,\n         \"multiplayer\": multiplayer},\n        session_id,\n    )\n    return {\n        \"architecture\": arch,\n        \"workflow\": \"gds/workflows/game-architecture.md\",\n        \"systems\": arch.get(\"systems\", []),\n    }\n"
}

Use to extend the system.\n\n    Args:\n        agent_name: Unique agent name\n        role: Agent's primary role\n        capabilities: List of capabilities\n        communication_style: How agent communicates\n        session_id: Optional session ID\n    \"\"\"\n    engine = get_engine()\n    workflow = engine.load_workflow(\"bmb\", \"workflows/create-agent\")\n    agent = await engine.execute_workflow(\n        workflow,\n        {\"agent_name\": agent_name, \"role\": role, \"capabilities\": capabilities,\n         \"communication_style\": communication_style},\n        session_id,\n    )\n    return {\n        \"agent_definition\": agent,\n        \"workflow\": \"bmb/workflows/create-agent.md\",\n        \"activation_command\": f\"/bmad-agent-{agent_name}\",\n    }\n\n\n@app.tool()\nasync def bmad_party_mode(\n    objective: str,\n    agents: list[str],\n    duration_minutes: int = 30,\n    session_id: str = \"\",\n) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Multi-agent collaboration mode. Use for complex tasks requiring multiple perspectives.\n\n    Args:\n        objective: What the group should accomplish\n        agents: List of agent names to include\n        duration_minutes: Time limit for session\n        session_id: Optional session ID\n    \"\"\"\n    engine = get_engine()\n    agent_personas = []\n    for agent_name in agents:\n        persona = engine.load_agent(agent_name)\n        if persona:\n            agent_personas.append(persona)\n    return {\n        \"party_session\": {\n            \"objective\": objective,\n            \"agents\": agent_personas,\n            \"duration\": duration_minutes,\n            \"facilitator_notes\": f\"Guide {len(agents)} agents to collaborate on: {objective}\",\n        },\n        \"workflow\": \"core/workflows/party-mode.md\",\n        \"agent_count\": len(agent_personas),\n        \"session_id\": session_id or engine.generate_session_id(),\n    }\n\n\n@app.tool()\nasync def bmad_list_workflows(\n    module: str = \"\",\n    phase: str = \"\",\n) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"List available BMAD workflows. Use to discover capabilities.\n\n    Args:\n        module: Filter by module (bmm/gds/cis/tea/bmb/core)\n        phase: Filter by phase (discover/plan/build/ship)\n    \"\"\"\n    engine = get_engine()\n    workflows = engine.list_workflows(module, phase)\n    return {\"workflows\": workflows, \"count\": len(workflows), \"modules\": engine.list_modules()}\n\n\n@app.tool()\nasync def bmad_list_agents(module: str = \"\") -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"List available BMAD agents. Use to see who can help.\n\n    Args:\n        module: Filter by module\n    \"\"\"\n    engine = get_engine()\n    agents = engine.list_agents(module)\n    return {\"agents\": agents, \"count\": len(agents), \"total_modules\": len(engine.list_modules())}\n\n\n@app.tool()\nasync def bmad_index_docs(\n    project_path: str = \".\",\n    include_patterns: list[str] | None = None,\n    session_id: str = \"\",\n) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Index project documentation. Use to make project searchable.\n\n    Args:\n        project_path: Path to project\n        include_patterns: File patterns to include\n        session_id: Optional session ID\n    \"\"\"\n    engine = get_engine()\n    result = await engine.index_project(\n        project_path, include_patterns or [\"*.md\", \"*.py\", \"*.js\", \"*.ts\", \"*.json\"],\n    )\n    return {\n        \"indexing_result\": result,\n        \"files_indexed\": result.get(\"files_indexed\", 0),\n        \"workflow\": \"core/tasks/index-docs.md\",\n    }\n\n\n@app.tool()\nasync def bmad_status(session_id: str = \"\") -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Get BMAD session status. Use to see current state.\n\n    Args:\n        session_id: Session ID to check\n    \"\"\"\n    session_mgr = get_session_manager()\n    if session_id:\n        session = await session_mgr.get_session(session_id)\n        return {\n            \"session\": session,\n            \"status\": \"active\" if session else \"not_found\",\n            \"history\": session.get(\"history\", []) if session else [],\n        }\n    engine = get_engine()\n    return {\n        \"modules_loaded\": engine.list_modules(),\n        \"agents_available\": len(engine.list_agents()),\n        \"workflows_available\": len(engine.list_workflows()),\n        \"redis_connected\": session_mgr.is_connected(),\n        \"version\": \"6.0.4\",\n    }\n"
}

  "content": "\"\"\"BMAD MCP Server - 108 BMAD commands via MCP protocol.\n\nUsage:\n    uv run python -m cohezion.mcp.bmad_server\n\nPorts:\n    - 8361: BMAD MCP Server (HTTP/SSE)\n\nEnvironment:\n    - MCP_API_KEY: Authentication key (required)\n    - REDIS_URL: Redis connection (default: redis://localhost:6379)\n    - BMAD_DATA_PATH: Path to _bmad directory (default: ./_bmad)\n    - MCP_PORT: Server port (default: 8361)\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport json\nimport os\n\nfrom cohezion.mcp.bmad_app import app, get_engine, logger, REDIS_URL, BMAD_DATA_PATH\n\n# Import tool modules to register @app.tool() decorators\nimport cohezion.mcp.bmad_tools  # noqa: F401\nimport cohezion.mcp.bmad_tools_ext  # noqa: F401\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# AGENT PROMPTS (28 agents as MCP prompts)\n# ============================================================================\n\n\n@app.prompt(name=\"bmad-pm\")\ndef bmad_pm_prompt() -> str:\n    \"\"\"BMAD Product Manager agent prompt.\"\"\"\n    engine = get_engine()\n    return engine.load_agent_prompt(\"bmm-pm\")\n\n\n@app.prompt(name=\"bmad-dev\")\ndef bmad_dev_prompt() -> str:\n    \"\"\"BMAD Developer agent prompt.\"\"\"\n    engine = get_engine()\n    return engine.load_agent_prompt(\"bmm-dev\")\n\n\n@app.prompt(name=\"bmad-architect\")\ndef bmad_architect_prompt() -> str:\n    \"\"\"BMAD Architect agent prompt.\"\"\"\n    engine = get_engine()\n    return engine.load_agent_prompt(\"bmm-architect\")\n\n\n@app.prompt(name=\"bmad-qa\")\ndef bmad_qa_prompt() -> str:\n    \"\"\"BMAD QA agent prompt.\"\"\"\n    engine = get_engine()\n    return engine.load_agent_prompt(\"bmm-qa\")\n\n\n@app.prompt(name=\"bmad-game-designer\")\ndef bmad_game_designer_prompt() -> str:\n    \"\"\"BMAD Game Designer agent prompt.\"\"\"\n    engine = get_engine()\n    return engine.load_agent_prompt(\"gds-game-designer\")\n\n\n@app.prompt(name=\"bmad-game-dev\")\ndef bmad_game_dev_prompt() -> str:\n    \"\"\"BMAD Game Developer agent prompt.\"\"\"\n    engine = get_engine()\n    return engine.load_agent_prompt(\"gds-game-dev\")\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# RESOURCES (Workflows, Agents, Documentation)\n# ============================================================================\n\n\n@app.resource(\"bmad://workflows/{module}/{workflow_id}\")\nasync def get_workflow_resource(module: str, workflow_id: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Get BMAD workflow content.\"\"\"\n    engine = get_engine()\n    result = engine.load_workflow(module, workflow_id)\n    if \"error\" in result:\n        return f\"# Error\\n{result['error']}\"\n    return result.get(\"content\", \"\")\n\n\n@app.resource(\"bmad://agents/{agent_name}\")\nasync def get_agent_resource(agent_name: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Get BMAD agent persona.\"\"\"\n    engine = get_engine()\n    content = engine.load_agent(agent_name)\n    return json.dumps(content, indent=2)\n\n\n@app.resource(\"bmad://modules\")\nasync def list_modules_resource() -> str:\n    \"\"\"List all BMAD modules.\"\"\"\n    engine = get_engine()\n    modules = engine.list_modules()\n    return json.dumps(modules, indent=2)\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# MAIN ENTRY\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Run the BMAD MCP server.\"\"\"\n    port = int(os.getenv(\"MCP_PORT\", \"8361\"))\n    transport = os.getenv(\"MCP_TRANSPORT\", \"http\")\n\n    logger.info(f\"Starting BMAD MCP Server v6.0.4 on port {port}\")\n    logger.info(f\"BMAD data path: {BMAD_DATA_PATH}\")\n    logger.info(f\"Redis URL: {REDIS_URL}\")\n    logger.info(f\"Transport: {transport}\")\n\n    if transport == \"stdio\":\n        app.run(transport=\"stdio\")\n    else:\n        app.run(host=\"0.0.0.0\", port=port, transport=\"http\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n"
}

    84→class PortAllocator:
    85→    """Manages port allocation for MCP servers."""
    86→
    87→    def __init__(self, port_range: range = PORT_RANGE):
    88→        self.port_range = port_range
    89→        self.allocated: dict[int, str] = {}  # port -> server_name
    90→
    91→    def allocate(self, server_name: str, preferred_port: int | None = None) -> int:
    92→        """Allocate a port for a server.
    93→
    94→        Args:
    95→            server_name: Name of the server
    96→            preferred_port: Preferred port (optional)
    97→
    98→        Returns:
    99→            Allocated port number
   100→        """
   101→        # Check if server already has a port
   102→        for port, name in self.allocated.items():
   103→            if name == server_name:
   104→                return port
   105→
   106→        # Try preferred port first
   107→        if preferred_port and preferred_port in self.port_range:
   108→            if preferred_port not in self.allocated:
   109→                self.allocated[preferred_port] = server_name
   110→                return preferred_port
   111→
   112→        # Find first available port
   113→        for port in self.port_range:
   114→            if port not in self.allocated:
   115→                self.allocated[port] = server_name
   116→                return port
   117→
   118→        raise RuntimeError(f"No available ports in range {self.port_range}")
   119→
   120→    def release(self, server_name: str) -> bool:
   121→        """Release a port allocated to a server."""
   122→        for port, name in list(self.allocated.items()):
   123→            if name == server_name:
   124→                del self.allocated[port]
   125→                return True
   126→        return False
   127→
   128→    def get_server_port(self, server_name: str) -> int | None:
   129→        """Get the port allocated to a server."""
   130→        for port, name in self.allocated.items():
   131→            if name == server_name:
   132→                return port
   133→        return None
   134→
   135→

   137→    """Manages all MCP servers."""
   138→
   139→    def __init__(self):

   141→        self.servers: dict[str, MCPServerConfig] = {}
   142→        self.health_check_task: asyncio.Task | None = None
   143→        self.metrics: dict[str, Any] = {}
   144→
   145→        # Ensure vault log directory exists
   146→        VAULT_LOG_PATH.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

   649→    app.add_routes(routes)
   650→
   651→    # Get manager and start servers
   652→    manager = get_manager()
   653→
   654→    # Start servers on startup
   655→    async def on_startup(app):
   656→        await manager.start_all()
   657→
   658→    # Stop servers on shutdown
   659→    async def on_shutdown(app):
   660→        await manager.stop_all()
   661→
   662→    app.on_startup.append(on_startup)
   663→    app.on_shutdown.append(on_shutdown)
   664→
   665→    # Run the server
   666→    logger.info(f"Starting MCP Server Manager on port {MANAGER_PORT}")
   667→    runner = web.AppRunner(app)
   668→    await runner.setup()
   669→    site = web.TCPSite(runner, "0.0.0.0", MANAGER_PORT)
   670→    await site.start()
   671→
   672→    # Keep running
   673→    while True:
   674→        await asyncio.sleep(3600)
   675→
   676→
   677→if __name__ == "__main__":
   678→    try:
   679→        asyncio.run(main())

   334→                ) as response,

   337→                if healthy:

   339→                return healthy
   340→        except Exception:

   350→                        healthy = await self.health_check(name)
   351→
   352→                        if not healthy:
   353→                            logger.warning(f"Server '{name}' is unhealthy")
   354→                            config.status = "unhealthy"
   355→
   356→                            if config.auto_restart and config.restart_count < config.max_restarts:
   357→                                logger.info(f"Auto-restarting server '{name}'")
   358→                                config.restart_count += 1
   359→                                await self.restart_server(name)
   360→                            else:
   361→                                logger.error(
   362→                                    f"Server '{name}' failed health check, not restarting (max restarts: {config.max_restarts})"
   363→                                )
   364→
   365→                await asyncio.sleep(30)  # Check every 30 seconds
   366→
   367→            except Exception as e:
   368→                logger.exception(f"Error in health check loop: {e}")
   369→                await asyncio.sleep(30)
   370→
   371→    def get_status(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
   372→        """Get status of all servers."""
   373→        return {
   374→            "manager": {
   375→                "port": MANAGER_PORT,
   376→                "servers_count": len(self.servers),
   377→                "port_range": f"{PORT_RANGE.start}-{PORT_RANGE.stop - 1}",
   378→            },
   379→            "servers": {name: config.to_dict() for name, config in self.servers.items()},
   380→        }
   381→
   382→    async def start_all(self):
   383→        """Start all registered servers."""
   384→        logger.info(f"Starting {len(self.servers)} MCP servers...")
   385→
   386→        for name in self.servers:
   387→            await self.start_server(name)
   388→
   389→        # Start health checking
   390→        self.health_check_task = asyncio.create_task(self.health_check_loop())
   391→
   392→    async def stop_all(self):
   393→        """Stop all servers."""
   394→        if self.health_check_task:
   395→            self.health_check_task.cancel()
   396→            with contextlib.suppress(asyncio.CancelledError):
   397→                await self.health_check_task
   398→
   399→        logger.info(f"Stopping {len(self.servers)} MCP servers...")
   400→
   401→        for name in self.servers:
   402→            await self.stop_server(name)
   403→
   404→
